Let's attempt derive a robust local method:
For current a (sum1), compute c=conv(a,a). Let M = max(c). We want reduce max. We can set active weights w_l = c_l^p for p>0, sum normalized. Then H= Conv_active. What about LP path more likely? Let's consider implementing LP for n=256 or 512 and use it as core. It may converge faster to low score. We can initialize with random and local multiplicative. We'll need to return best; can run both and choose best.

But LP path with all constraints may be expensive; we can use it for several lengths/restarts. Let's design LP update in detail:
Function lp_direction(a, M=None):
- n=len(a); if M None M=max_conv(a) * (1+eps) to allow slight. Actually if M=max(a*a) exactly, constraints feasible b=0. To maximize sum, b positive; okay. Use M slightly > to avoid numerical.
- Build A_ub (m x n). We can optimize by using scipy.sparse? linprog accepts sparse. Build csr? We can create using scipy.sparse diags? The matrix A is a Hankel/Toeplitz convolution: A_{l,j}=a_{l-j} if 0≤l-j≤n-1. This is a banded Toeplitz? Rows are sliding windows of a reversed. Can build dense easily for n≤512.
- constraints: A_ub b ≤ M*ones. b≥0. Objective c=-ones maximize sum.
- Use linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(m,M), bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs'). To improve scaling, M maybe ~2; b can large. Fine.
- The returned b may be all zero if constraints too tight? Since b=0 feasible but objective optimum might be zero if any M=0; but M>0. There is always positive optimum? For a with some zeros maybe. If b.sum() too small, fallback h.
- Normalize b to sum=1.
But if M is max(a*a), the constraints a*b≤M may allow b with huge entries where a small. If a has zeros, b can be unbounded! Example a_0=0? Constraint rows? If a has zero on all support? Sum a=1 so not. But if a_i=0 for some i, b_j may be multiplied by zero in many rows; constraints involving b_j have coefficients a_{l-j}=a_i. If a_i small, coefficient small for all rows where i matches, so b_j can be large -> LP unbounded? Let's check: coefficient of b_j in row l is a_{l-j} (if in range). For fixed j, as l varies, coefficient is a_{i}. It is zero if a_i=0, but nonzero for i in [l-j] where 0≤i<n. In particular row l=j gives coefficient a_0; if a0=0, but some other l gives a_i>0. Multiplicative optimizer routine:
```
def multiplicative_optimize(a, rng, iterations, budget, base_step, top_frac):
  n=len(a); S=a.sum(); a=a/S*sqrt(2n)
  best=a.copy(); best_score=maxconv(a)
  N2=fft_size(3*n-2)
  z=np.log(np.clip(a,1e-30,None))
  step = base_step
  no_improve=0
  for it in range(iters until time):
     c=fast_conv(a,N?); M=max(c)
     if M<best_score: update best, no_improve=0
     else no_improve++
     # select active weights: top K maybe
     K=max(8, int(n*top_frac))
     thr = np.sort(c)[::-1][K-1]
     excess = np.maximum(c - thr,0)
     if sum small: active indices = top K; w uniform
     else w = excess; normalize
     # optionally randomize weights: apply cvx.random powers
     H = hankel_active(a,w)
     # gradient direction log: g = H - mean(H) (to keep sum roughly)
     g = H
     # scale step: maxg = max(g); eta = step / max(1e-30,maxg)
     # candidate log = z - eta*g (or minus H/meanH*step? )
     z_new = z - (step / maxg) * (g / maxg)  # changes up to -step for largest contributors
     # add random noise proportional to noise_amp
     z_new += rng.normal(0, noise_amp, n)
     cand = np.exp(z_new)
     cand = np.clip(cand,0,None)
     cand_sum=cand.sum(); if cand_sum<1e-12 fail
     cand = cand / cand_sum * S
     score = maxfast(cand)
     if score < best_score + tolerance: accept (or if score < current score for local)
     else reduce step, maybe revert z
```
This is like noisy gradient. Accept if improves current? For local, keep current if score lower than current_score; update best if best. If reject, reduce step. If stuck, increase noise/reset.

Because q nonconvex, step too large can worsen. Use line search: try λ (i.e., reduce step) [1,0.5,0.25,...] until improvement. The relative update in log: z_new = z - step * H / scale (scale=mean(H) maybe). Since scale of H ~ M? If H mean ~ M? Use scale=max(H). We can set z_new = z - alpha * H / max(H). alpha starting 0.1-1.0. Candidate sum altered; renormalize.
We can implement for candidate alpha in [alpha, alpha/2,...] until score < current_score or alpha tiny. If no improvement, reset z? current remains. Increase noise? Maybe use random active sets to escape.

But this per-iteration uses multiple FFTs for line search, slow. Could accept first if improves current (not best), else reduce. We can maintain current_score.

Alternatively use fixed small step and simulated annealing accept worse. We can tune.

LP strategy maybe more directed. Let's not overcomplicate yet.

Implementation details for hankel_active via FFT:
```
def hankel_active(a, w, size=None):
    n=a.size
    if size is None: size = _fft_size(3*n-2)
    a_rev = a[::-1]
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a_rev, size)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, size)
    d = np.fft.irfft(fa*fw, size)
    return d[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Check length: a_rev n, w 2n-1, conv len 3n-2; slice n-1 to 2n-1 inclusive length n. d length size. Need ensure no wrap: size >=3n-2; next power2 okay.
Test n=2 earlier gave H0 at D[1], H1 at D[2]. For n=2, n-1=1, end 2, works. Great.

Fast convolution:
```
def fast_conv(a, size=None):
    n=a.size
    if size is None: size = _fft_size(2*n-1)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
    cc=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,size)
    return cc[:2*n-1]
```

Potential numerical: irfft can have tiny negatives; max unaffected. For c weights, use np.maximum(c,0).

Algorithm `_improve_mir(a, rng, budget, p_start, p_end, step, noise, kick)`:
- n,len; Nc,Nh; scale S.
- z=log(a)
- current_score=maxfast
- best_total_score = current_score; for global pass? We'll update global best in main or inside via return best.

  - c=maxfast? use conv

  - H = hankel_active(a,w,Nh)
  - g=H - np.dot(a,H)/S
  - maxg = max(abs(g)); if maxg<1e-12: do random kick
  - dir = g / maxg
  - try alphas = [step, step*0.5, step*0.25, step*0.125]
    for alpha in alphas:
       zc = z - alpha*dir + noise*rng.normal? We can add noise once or each? For search, add small noise to candidate. If we add different noise per alpha, okay. Keep base noise array? To make line search deterministic, generate noise = noise_amp*normal once; candidate zc = z - alpha*dir + noise.
       cand=exp(zc); cand=cand/sum*S; score=maxfast(cand)
       if score < current_score: accept break
  - if accepted: current_score=score; z=zc; no_improve=0; maybe step=step*1.05 up to max; p? If rejected: step*=0.7; no_improve++
  - if no_improve > streak_limit (e.g., 40): random kick. Kick: z += kick_amp * rng.normal(0,1,n); z -= mean(z) (preserve scale) maybe; cand=exp(z); scale; current_score=maxfast(cand). Accept kick regardless? It may worsen current; we can set z=cand, current_score=...; if worse than best, okay; then step small. To avoid losing best, best kept. Kick amp maybe 0.1-0.3. Or if no improvement long, reinitialize from best with noise.
  - if current_score > best_score * 1.5 maybe revert to best and kick.
- Return best.

Let's think about LP implementation and integration.

LP helper:
```
def _lp_direction(a):
   n=len(a); m=2*n-1; S=a.sum(); M=max_conv(a)* (1+1e-8)+1e-6
   A=np.zeros((m,n), dtype=float)
   # fill
   for l in range(n): A[l,:l+1]=a[l::-1]
   for l in range(n,m):
       start=l-n+1

def _next_pow2(m):
    p=1
    while p < m: p <<=1
    return p
```

Let's define:
```
def _fast_convolution(a, size):
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    return np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, size)[:2*a.size-1]
```
For max only, we can `np.max` and not store? Still store. Could compute in place? fine.

Active weights:
```
def _make_weights(c, power, rng, scale=1.0):
    cc = np.maximum(c,0.0)
    if np.max(cc) <= 0:
       w=np.ones_like(cc)
    else:
       cc = cc / np.max(cc)
       # if power large, underflow edges; add eps
       w = cc ** power
    # add small random to break symmetry
    # But if w has zeros, random tiny can influence H? If w sum large, random negligible. Maybe add 1e-8.
    w = w + 1e-12 * rng.random(cc.size)
    s=w.sum()
    if s <=0: w=np.ones_like(cc)/cc.size else w/=s
    return w
```
Power schedule in mir. Since c normalized, c**8 okay. If c all zero (impossible S>0).

Mir update detailed:
```
def _mir_update(a, rng, power, step, noise):
   n=len(a); S=a.sum()
   c = _fast_convolution(a, _next_pow2(2*n-1))
   M = float(c.max())
   w = _make_weights(c, power, rng)
   H = _hankel_active(a, w, _next_pow2(3*n-2))
   g = H - np.dot(a,H)/S
   maxg = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
   if maxg < 1e-14:
      # random kick
      z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
      z += noise * rng.standard_normal(n)
      z -= z.mean()
      b=np.exp(z); b=_scale_to(b,S)
      return b, _fast_max(b), True
   direction = g / maxg
   noise_vec = noise * rng.standard_normal(n)
   return direction, noise_vec, M
```
But to save FFT, in main compute c and max. H uses w. We can combine.

def _improve_mirror(a, rng, time_limit, p_start, p_end, step_init, noise_init, kick_thresh=30):
   n=len; S=np.sqrt(2*n)
   a=_scale_to(a,S)
   Nc=_next_pow2(2*n-1); Nh=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
   z=np.log(np.clip(a,1e-300,None)); z-=z.mean()
   a=np.exp(z); a=_scale_to(a,S)
   current_score = _fast_max(a,Nc)
   best=a.copy(); best_score=current_score
   step=step_init; noise=noise_init; power=p_start
   no_improve=0; kick_count=0
   t_end=time.monotonic()+time_limit
   while time.monotonic()<t_end:
      c=_fast_convolution(a,Nc)
      cur=float(c.max())
      if cur < best_score - 1e-12:
          best=a.copy(); best_score=cur; print(...)
      if abs(cur-current_score)>1e-9: current_score=cur
      # update power
      power = min(p_end, power + 0.01) maybe
      w=_make_weights(c,power,rng)
      H=_hankel_active(a,w,Nh)
      g=H - np.dot(a,H)/S
      maxg=float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
      if maxg < 1e-12:
          # random kick
          z = np.log(np.clip(a,1e-300,None)); z -= z.mean()
          z += noise * rng.standard_normal(n)
          z -= z.mean()
          a = _scale_to(np.exp(z),S); z=np.log(np.clip(a,1e-300,None)); z-=z.mean(); current_score=_fast_max(a,Nc); step=step_init*0.5; no_improve=0; continue
      direction = g / maxg
      accepted=False
      alphas = [step, step*0.5, step*0.25, step*0.125, step*0.0625]
      nv = noise * rng.standard_normal(n) # one noise
      for alpha in alphas:
          zc = z - alpha * direction + nv
          zc -= zc.mean()  # optional
          cand = np.exp(zc)
          cand = _scale_to(cand, S)
          if cand is None: continue
          score = _fast_max(cand, Nc)
          if np.isfinite(score) and score < current_score:
              z = zc; a = cand; current_score = score; step = min(1.0, step*1.05); no_improve=0; accepted=True
              break
          # If alpha smaller maybe try
      if not accepted:
          step *= 0.7
          no_improve += 1
          if no_improve > kick_thresh:
              # kick from best or current? use current plus larger noise
              z = np.log(np.clip(a,1e-300,None)); z -= z.mean()
              kick = 0.1 + 0.2*(kick_count%3)
              z += kick * rng.standard_normal(n); z -= z.mean()
              a = _scale_to(np.exp(z), S)
              z=np.log(np.clip(a,1e-300,None)); z-=z.mean()
              current_score = _fast_max(a,Nc)
              step = step_init
              no_improve=0
              kick_count +=1
              if current_score > best_score*1.5:
                  # revert to best and kick smaller
                  a=best.copy(); z=... noise small
                  current_score=best_score
      # Decay noise over time? Maybe noise = max(0.001, noise_init*0.999)
      noise = max(0.005, noise_init * 0.9995)
   return best,best_score
```

`_convolve_matrix(a)`:
```
def _convolve_matrix(a):
    n=a.size; m=2*n-1
    A=np.empty((m,n), dtype=np.float64)
    # first n rows
    for l in range(n):
        A[l,:l+1]=a[l::-1]
    # second
    for l in range(n,m):
        start=l-n+1
        A[l,start:n]=a[n-1:start-1:-1]
    return A
```

def _lp_direction(a):
    n=a.size; S=a.sum()
    M = _fast_max(a,_next_pow2(2*n-1))
    M = max(M, 1e-6)*(1+1e-6)+1e-12
    A=_convolve_matrix(a)
    b=np.ones(n,n? )? null
    if linprog is None: return None
    cap = S  # max possible due sum
    bounds = [(0.0, cap)]*n
    res=linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(2*n-1,M), bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve': True, 'dual_feasibility_tolerance':1e-7})
    if res is None or not res.success or res.x is None:
       # fallback: solve least squares? maybe use a
       return None
    b=np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
    b=np.clip(b,0,cap)
    bsum=b.sum()
    if bsum <= 1e-9:
       # If LP failed, use reciprocal of a? g=1/(a+eps) normalized?
       g = 1.0/(a+1e-6*a.mean()+1e-12); return _scale_to(g,S)
    g=_scale_to(b,S)
    if g is None: return None
    # add tiny noise
    g *= np.exp(0.01*rng?) no rng param; maybe deterministic
    return g
```

def _improve_lp(a, rng, limit):
    n=len; if n>800: return a,score
    S=...; a=scale

def _initial_seeds(n, rng):
   seeds=[]
   seeds.append(np.ones(n))
   # random smooth sine
   phases = rng.uniform(-np.pi,np.pi, 5); freqs = rng.integers(1,8,5); amps = rng.uniform(0.2,0.8,5)
   x = 1.0
   for f,p,a in zip(freqs,phases,amps): x += a*np.sin(2*np.pi*f*np.arange(n)/n + p)
   x = np.maximum(x,1e-3); seeds.append(x)
   # random walk log
   z = np.cumsum(rng.normal(0,0.7,n)); z -= np.mean(z); z = np.clip(z,-2,2); z -= np.mean(z); seeds.append(np.exp(z))
   # block
   block= max(8, n//128); val = np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.5, block)); tiled=np.tile(val, n//block+1)[:n]; seeds append
   # cosine hill? a=0.2+exp(-((i-c)/width)^2)
   # parent if available maybe
```

def _upscale_sequence(a, n_new):
    if len(a)==n_new: return copy
    # linear interpolation of normalized sequence? Use np.interp on indices [0,n-1] to [0,n_new-1]
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,len(a)); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n_new)
    b=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
    b=np.maximum(b,1e-6); return b
```

def _convolve_row(a, l):
    n=a.size
    row=np.zeros(n)
    if l < n:
       row[:l+1]=a[l::-1]
    else:
       start=l-n+1
       row[start:n]=a[n-1:start-1:-1]
    return row
```

def _improve_lp(a, rng, time_limit, top_frac=0.5):
    n=a.size
    if n>1024 or linprog is None: return a, _fast_max(...)
    S=...; a=scale; Nc=...
    current_score = _fast_max(a,Nc)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=current_score
    step_iter=0; no_improve=0
    end=time.monotonic()+time_limit
    while time.monotonic()<end:
       # maybe every 30 no improve restart
       g=_lp_direction(a, rng, max_constraints=min(2*n-1, max(128, n)))
       if g is None:
          # random kick
          ...)
       else:
          # line search candidates
          inds=[0.4,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001]
          best_cand=None; best_sc=current_score
          for lam in inds:
             cand=(1-lam)*a + lam*g
             cand = _scale_to(cand,S)
             if cand is None: continue
             sc = _fast_max(cand,Nc)
             if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc=sc; best_cand=cand
          if best_cand is not None:
             a=best_cand; current_score=best_sc; no_improve=0; if <best update
             # if improved, maybe reduce lam? no
          else:
             no_improve += 1
             if no_improve > 8:
                # restart from best with random perturbation
                kick = 0.1 + 0.1*min(no_improve,4)
                z=log(best); z += kick*normal; z-=mean; a=scale(exp(z)); current_score=fastmax; no_improve=0
             elif no_improve>3:
                # perturb a slightly
                z=log(a); z += 0.05*normal; ...
    return best,best_score
```

def _better_sequence(best_seq, best_score, candidate, score):
    if candidate is None or score is None: return best_seq,best_score
    if not np.isfinite(score): return best_seq,best_score
    if best_seq is None or score < best_score:
        return candidate.copy(), float(score)
    return best_seq,best_score
```

Now, code details for random start functions:
```
def _constant_start(n, rng=None):
   return np.ones(n)  # scaled later

def _smooth_start(n, rng):
   t = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
   a = np.ones(n)
   for _ in range(4):
       f = float(rng.integers(1, 9))
       phi = float(rng.uniform(-np.pi, np.pi))
       amp = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 0.8))
       a = a * np.exp(amp * np.sin(2*np.pi*f*t/n + phi))
   return np.maximum(a, 1e-3)
```
This maintains positive.
`_random_walk_start`:
```
z = np.zeros(n)
for i in range(1,n): z[i] = z[i-1]+rng.normal(0,0.7)
z = np.clip(z, -2,2)
z -= z.mean()
return np.exp(0.8*z)
```
`_block_start`:
```
block=max(4,n//200)
m=ceil(n/block)
vals=np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.6,m))
b=np.repeat(vals, block)[:n]
return np.maximum(b,1e-3)
```
`_interpolate_start(base,n,new)`: if base len maybe different.
```
x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,base.size)
x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
b=np.interp(x_new,x_old,base)
b=np.maximum(b,1e-6); return b
```

```
def _generate_starts(n, rng, parent=None):
  starts=[]
  if parent is not None:
     if parent.size == n: a=parent.copy()
     else: a = _interpolate_start(parent,n,n)
     starts.append(a)
  starts.append(np.ones(n))
  for _ in range(3):
     starts.append(_random_walk_start(n,rng))
     starts.append(_smooth_start(n,rng))
     starts.append(_block_start(n,rng))
  # scale each? Return unscaled; caller scales.
```

```

import numpy as np
import time
try:

TARGET_SCORE = 1.5027

def _clip_array(arr): ...

`_clip_array`:
```
def _clip_array(x):
    arr = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return arr
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return arr
```

`_scale_to`:
```
def _scale_to(a, target_sum):
    a = np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size==0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
       finite = np.isfinite(a); if not any... return None
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, ...)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-300:
       return None
    return a * (target_sum / s)
```

`_fast_max`:
```
def _fast_max(a, size):
    a = np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size==0: return np.inf
    n=a.size
    if size is None: size=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, size=size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, n=size)
    c = c[:2*n-1]
    m=float(np.max(c))
    if m < 0 and m > -1e-9: m=0.0
    return m
```

    n=a.size
    m=_fast_max(a,None)
    return float(2*n*m/(s*s))
```

`_make_weights`:
```
def _make_weights(c, power, rng):
    c = np.asanarray(c)
    if c.size ==0: return np.zeros(0)
    cc = np.maximum(c,0.0)
    mx = float(np.max(cc))
    if mx <= 0:
        w = np.ones(cc.size, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        cc = cc / mx
        # to avoid underflow for high power, maybe clip lower 0
        w = cc ** power
    # add tiny random baseline
    if rng is not None:
        w = w + 1e-9 * rng.random(cc.size)
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if s <= 0:
        w = np.ones(cc.size)/cc.size
    else:
        w = w / s
    return w
```
If power up to16, cc**16 underflows for many; okay.

`_hankel_active` size:
```
def _hankel_active(a,w,size):
    n=a.size
    if size is None: size=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
    ar=a[::-1]
    fa=np.fft.rfft(ar,size)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w,size)
    d=np.fft.irfft(fa*fw,n=size)
    return d[n-1:2*n-1]
```
If w size not 2n-1? It should. If w from c which is 2n-1. Good.

`_improve_mirror`: Let's code robustly.
```
def _improve_mirror(a_init, rng, time_limit, p_start=3.0, p_end=10.0, step_init=0.18, noise_init=0.04):
    a = _clip_array(a_init)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return a, np.inf
    S = float(np.sqrt(2.0*n))
    a = _scale_to(a, S)
    if a is None:
       a=np.ones(n); a*=S/n
    Nc=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    Nh=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    a = _scale_to(a,S)
    z = np.log(a)
    z -= float(np.mean(z))
    a = np.exp(z); a = _scale_to(a,S)
    current_score = _fast_max(a,Nc)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=current_score
    step=step_init; noise=noise_init; power=p_start
    no_improve=0; kick=0
    temp=0.15
    end=time.monotonic()+max(0.0,time_limit)
    iteration=0
    while time.monotonic() < end:
       iteration +=1
       c = _fast_convolution(a,Nc)
       cur = float(np.max(c))
       if cur < best_score - 1e-12:
           best = a.copy(); best_score = cur
           if iteration % 50 ==0 or best_score < TARGET_SCORE + 0.1:
               print(f"mirror n={n} iter={iteration} best={best_score:.7f}")
       if abs(cur - current_score) > 1e-10:
           current_score = cur
       # update power temp noise
       if power < p_end:
           power = min(p_end, power + 0.005)
       temp = 0.15 * (1.0 - 0.5 * min(1.0, iteration/3000.0))  # decays to0.075? maybe
       noise = max(0.002, noise_init * (1.0 - 0.5*min(1.0, iteration/5000.0)))
       w = _make_weights(c, power, rng)
       H = _hankel_active(a,w,Nh)
       mu = float(np.dot(a,H))/S
       g = H - mu
       maxg = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
       if maxg < 1e-13:
           z = np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-12)); z -= z.mean()
           z += 0.1 * rng.standard_normal(n)
           z = np.clip(z, -25,25); z -= z.mean()
           cand = _scale_to(np.exp(z), S)
           if cand is not None:
               a=cand; current_score=_fast_max(a,Nc)
           step=step_init; no_improve=0; continue
       direction = g / maxg
       nv = noise * rng.standard_normal(n)
       best_trial = None; best_trial_score = np.inf
       alphas = (step, step*0.5, step*0.25, step*0.125, step*0.0625)
       for alpha in alphas:
           zc = z - alpha * direction + nv
           zc = np.clip(zc, -25, 25)
           zc -= float(np.mean(zc))
           cand = np.exp(zc)
           cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
           cand = _scale_to(cand, S)
           if cand is None: continue
           sc = _fast_max(cand, Nc)
           if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_trial_score:
               best_trial_score = sc; best_trial = cand
       if best_trial is not None:
           threshold = current_score + temp * max(1.0, best_score)
           if best_trial_score < threshold:
               z = np.log(np.maximum(best_trial,1e-12)); z -= z.mean()
               a = _scale_to(np.exp(z), S)
               current_score = _fast_max(a, Nc)
               if best_trial_score < current_score + 1e-9:
                   current_score = best_trial_score
               if best_trial_score <= current_score + 1e-9: # accepted maybe within temp
                   no_improve += 1
                   if best_trial_score < best_score:
                       no_improve = 0
               step = min(0.6, step * 1.03)
               # If currently far worse than best and temp allowed but not improving, snap back occasionally
               if current_score > best_score * 1.25 and no_improve > 20:
                   a = best.copy(); z = np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-12)); z-=z.mean(); current_score=best_score; no_improve=0; step=step_init
               continue
       # rejected
       step *= 0.75
       no_improve += 1
       if no_improve > 50:
           kick += 1
           amp = 0.08 * min(kick, 5)
           z = np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-12)); z-=z.mean()
           z += amp * rng.standard_normal(n)
           z = np.clip(z,-25,25); z-=z.mean()
           a = _scale_to(np.exp(z), S)
           z = np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-12)); z-=z.mean()
           current_score = _fast_max(a, Nc)
           step = step_init
           no_improve = 0
    final_score = _fast_score(best)
    return best, final_score
```

def _improve_lp(a_init, rng, time_limit, n_lp=None, top_constraints=None):
   a = _clip_array(a_init); n=a.size; if n>1200: return a, _fast_score(a)
   S=...
   Nc=...
   a=scale
   current_score=_fast_max(a,Nc)
   best=a.copy(); best_score=current_score
   no_improve=0; end=...
   if top_constraints is None:
       if n<=512: top_constraints=2*n-1
       else: top_constraints=min(2*n-1, n)
   while time<end:
      c = _fast_convolution(a,Nc)
      idx = None
      if top_constraints < 2*n-1:
          idx = np.argpartition(c, -top_constraints)[-top_constraints:]
      else:
          idx = np.arange(2*n-1)
      g = _lp_direction_from_rows(a, idx, rng, S)
      if g is not None:
         best_cand=None; best_sc=current_score
         # We can use c max? line search lam
         lams = (0.3,0.15,0.075,0.04,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001)
         for lam in lams:
             cand=(1-lam)*a + lam*g
             cand=np.maximum(cand,0.0)
             cand=_scale_to(cand,S)
             if cand is None: continue
             sc=_fast_max(cand,Nc)
             if np.isfinite(sc) and sc<best_sc:
                 best_sc=sc; best_cand=cand
         if best_cand is not None:
             a=best_cand; current_score=best_sc; no_improve=0
             if current_score<best_score: best=a.copy(); best_score=current_score; print
             continue
      no_improve +=1
      if no_improve > 6:
          # random restart from best with perturbation
          amp=0.1 + 0.05*min(no_improve,5)
          z=np.log(np.maximum(best,1e-12)); z-=mean; z += amp*rng.normal
          z=clip; a=scale(exp(z)); current_score=fastmax; no_improve=0
      else:
          # small random perturbation current
          z=np.log(max(a)); z-=mean; z +=0.03*rng.normal; ...
   return best, _fast_score(best)

LP direction helper:
```
def _lp_direction_from_rows(a, idx, rng, S):
   n=a.size
   if linprog is None: return None
   M = None? We need M for b_ub. Could compute max conv c? We can pass M from LP loop to avoid recompute. Modify `_improve_lp` to pass M=max(c).
   A = _convolve_matrix_rows(a, idx)
   b_ub = M * np.ones(len(idx))
   cap = S
   c_obj = -np.ones(n)
   bounds = [(0.0, cap.0)] * n
   try:
      res = linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve': True, 'max_iter': 20000})
   except Exception:
      return None
   x = None
   if res is not None and res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
       x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
   if x is None:
       # fallback: inverse of a
       x = 1.0 / (np.maximum(a,1e-12)+0.1*(S/n))
   x = np.clip(x, 0.0, cap)
   s = x.sum()
   if s <= 1e-12:
       return None
   g = x / s * S
   # add small multiplicative noise
   if rng is not None:
      g = g * np.exp(0.02 * rng.standard_normal(n))
   g = _scale_to(g,S)
   return g
```
`bounds` list with upper `cap` (float). Good. If `cap` zero? S>0.

If `linprog` returns status 'Iteration limit reached' but x valid, maybe use it? The code uses x even if not res.success. That's okay as heuristic. But if x violates constraints, line search exact may still improve/not. Fine.

`_convolve_matrix_rows` with idx unsorted; A dense. Top constraints maybe int array. Need if `idx` is None? no.

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 6.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    cands = []
    best_seq = None
    best_score = np.inf

    parent = _get_parent()
    # choose main length
    if parent is not None and parent.size >= 256 and parent.size <= 8192:
        n_main = int(parent.size)
    else:
        n_main = 4096
    # maybe cap if parent >8192 to 4096
    # generate starts
    starts = _generate_starts(n_main, rng, parent)
    S_main = sqrt(2*n_main)
    Nc_main = _next_pow2(2*n_main-1)
    best_start = None; best_start_score=inf
    for st in starts:
        st = _scale_to(st, S_main)
        if st is None: st=np.ones(n_main)*(S_main/n_main)
        sc = _fast_score(st) # or fast_max
        if sc < best_start_score:
           best_start=st; best_start_score=sc
    if best_start is None: best_start=...
    cands = _add_candidate(cands, best_start, best_start_score, 8)
    if best_start_score < best_score: best_seq=best_start.copy(); best_score=best_start_score

    # Phase 1 mirror on main if time>150
    tl = min(_time_left(deadline), 900.0)
    if tl > 30:
        seq, score = _improve_mirror(best_start, rng, tl, ...)
        cands = _add_candidate(cands, seq, score, 8)
        if score < best_score: best_seq=seq.copy(); best_score=score; print
    # exact check maybe later

    # Phase 2 LP small
    if _time_left(deadline) > 350:
       n_lp=512
       a_random = _scale_to(_random_walk_start(n_lp,rng), sqrt(2*n_lp))
       seq,score=_improve_lp(a_random, rng, min(_time_left(deadline)-10, 300.0))
       update
    # Phase 3 LP 1024
    if _time_left(deadline) > 300 and linprog is not None:
       n_lp=1024
       # use maybe best LP 512 upscaled? Simpler random walk + smooth. Use a random start from generate top?
       starts_lp = _generate_starts(n_lp, rng, None)
       choose best start by fast_score
       seq,score=_improve_lp(...)
       update
    # Phase 4 final mirror remaining
    rem = _time_left(deadline)
    if rem > 120:
       # choose base: best_seq if not None else best_start
       base = best_seq if best_seq is not None else best_start
       if base.size != n_main:
           base = _interpolate_sequence(base, n_main)
       base = _scale_to(base, sqrt(2*n_main))
       # Also maybe fresh random large
       # Run mirror on base for min(rem-20, 600?) maybe all.
       tl = max(0.0, rem - 30.0)
       seq,score = _improve_mirror(base, rng, tl)
       update
       # If still time >200, run mirror on a fresh random large
       if _time_left(deadline) > 200:
          fresh = _scale_to(_random_walk_start(n_main,rng), sqrt(2*n_main))
          seq2,score2=_improve_mirror(fresh,rng, _time_left(deadline)-30)
          update
    # final exact selection
    # sort cands? We'll loop to find min internal if not exact.
    # exact check top candidates within deadline
    chosen_seq = best_seq; chosen_exact = np.inf
    # To prioritize, maybe sort cands by internal score manually into list sorted
    top = list(cands)
    # simple selection sort by score to process lowest first
    for i in range(len(top)):
       minj=i
       for j in range(i+1,len(top)): if top[j][1] < top[minj][1]: minj=j
       if minj!=i: swap
    for item in top:
       if _time_left(deadline) <= 10: break
       if item[0].size > 10000: continue
       es = _exact_check(item[0])
       if np.isfinite(es):
           if es < chosen_exact or chosen_seq is None:
               chosen_seq = item[0]; chosen_exact = es
       # we can break after 3 exact? but if lower exact maybe. Process max 4.
    if chosen_seq is None:
       chosen_seq = best_seq if best_seq is not None else np.ones(256)
    # Ensure valid and final maybe exact if chosen_seq not exact checked? If chosen_seq size<=10000 and time, check and if exact lower? not needed.
    # If best_seq size <=10000 and not exact-checked but chosen from exact maybe? We can final exact chosen if time>5.
    if chosen_seq.size <= 12000 and _time_left(deadline)>5:
       es = _exact_check(chosen_seq)
       if np.isfinite(es):
           chosen_exact=es
    arr = _clip_array(chosen_seq)
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
    if arr.size ==0 or np.sum(arr) <=0:
       arr = np.ones(256)
    return [float(x) for x in arr]
```

Let's code `_improve_lp` carefully:
```
def _improve_lp(a_init, rng, time_limit, top_constraints=None):
    a = _clip_array(a_init)
    n=a.size
    if n==0 or n>1500:
       return a, _fast_score(a)
    S=...
    Nc=...
    a = np.maximum(a,1e-12); a=_scale_to(a,S)
    current_score = _fast_max(a,Nc)
    best = a.copy(); best_score=current_score
    no_improve=0; end=...
    if top_constraints is None:
       if n <= 512: top_constraints = 2*n-1
       else: top_constraints = min(2*n-1, max(256, n//2))
    while time < end:
       c = _fast_convolution(a,Nc)
       cur = float(np.max(c))
       if cur < best_score -1e-12:
          best=a.copy(); best_score=cur; print
       if abs(cur-current_score)>1e-10: current_score=cur
       if top_constraints < 2*n-1:
          idx = np.argpartition(c, -top_constraints)[-top_constraints:]
          # maybe ensure unique? argpartition returns unique.
       else:
          idx = np.arange(2*n-1)
       g = _lp_direction_from_rows(a, idx, current_score, rng, S)
       if g is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
           g = None
       if g is not None:
          accepted=False
          best_cand=None; best_sc=current_score
          lams=(0.35,0.18,0.09,0.045,0.025,0.015,0.008,0.004,0.002)
          for lam in lams:
             cand = (1-lam)*a + lam*g
             cand = np.maximum(cand,0.0)
             cand = _scale_to(cand,S)
             if cand is None: continue
             sc = _fast_max(cand,Nc)
             if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc=sc; best_cand=cand
          if best_cand is not None and best_sc < current_score:
             a=best_cand; current_score=best_sc; no_improve=0; accepted=True
             if current_score < best_score:
                best=a.copy(); best_score=current_score; print
       if not accepted:
          no_improve +=1
          if no_improve > 5:
             # restart from best with noise
             amp = 0.08 + 0.03*min(no_improve,5)
             z=np.log(np.maximum(best,1e-12)); z-=mean; z += amp*rng.normal; z=clip; a=scale(exp); current_score=fastmax; no_improve=0
          else:
             z=... small noise current; update current_score? If worse, no_improve remains? maybe set a to perturbed and current_score=...; no_improve reset? To avoid immediate repeat, yes reset? But if worse, can get stuck. Use no_improve = max(1,no_improve-2).
    final=_fast_score(best)
    return best, final
```

def _sort_candidates(cands):
   arr = list(cands)
   m=len(arr)
   for i in range(m-1):
       minj=i
       minsc=arr[i][1]
       for j in range(i+1,m):
          if arr[j][1] < minsc:
             minj=j; minsc=arr[j][1]
       if minj != i:
          tmp=arr[i]; arr[i]=arr[minj]; arr[minj]=tmp
   return arr
```

def _anti_palindromic_start(n, rng):
    a=np.ones(n)
    if n%2==0:
       left = 0.2 + 1.8*rng.random(n//2)
       a[:n//2]=left
       a[n//2:]=2.0 - left[::-1] # complement around 1
    else: a[n//2]=0.1
    return np.maximum(a,0.05)
```

Detailed code:

```
"""
Searches for nonnegative sequences minimizing evaluate_sequence score.
Uses normalized Fast-FFT objective, mirror/peak-gradient local search, and LP direction iterations inspired by SOTA.
"""
import numpy as np
import time
try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None

TARGET_SCORE = 1.5027
```

Helper `_time_left`:
```
def _time_left(deadline):
    return float(max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic()))
```

`_next_pow2`:
```
def _next_pow2(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    p=1
    while p < int(m): p <<=1
    return p
```

`_clip_array` as above.

`_scale_to`:
```
def _scale_to(a, target_sum):
    a = _clip_array(a)
    if a.size==0: return None
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-300: return None
    return a * (float(target_sum) / s)
```

`_fast_convolution`:
```
def _fast_convolution(a, size):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.zeros(0)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, n=size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, n=size)
    return c[:2*n-1]
```
Need size int. If size maybe less than 2n-1? We pass correct.

`_fast_max`:
```
def _fast_max(a, size=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.inf
    if size is None or size < 2*n-1: size=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    c=_fast_convolution(a,size)
    m=float(np.max(c))
    if m < 0.0 and m > -1e-8: m=0.0
    return m
```

`_fast_score`:
```
def _fast_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.inf
    s=float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s): return np.inf
    m=_fast_max(a,None)
    if not np.isfinite(m): return np.inf
    return float(2.0*n*m/(s*s))
```

`_hankel_active`:
```
def _hankel_active(a, w, size=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64); w=np.asarray(w,dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.zeros(0)
    if size is None or size < 3*n-2: size=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
    ar=a[::-1]
    fa=np.fft.rfft(ar,n=size)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w,n=size)
    d=np.fft.irfft(fa*fw,n=size)
    return d[n-1:2*n-1]
```
If w length not 2n-1? It should. If longer, size maybe. okay.

`_make_weights`:
```
def _make_weights(c, power, rng):
    c=np.asarray(c,dtype=np.float64)
    m=c.size
    if m==0: return np.zeros(0)
    cc=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    mx=float(np.max(cc))
    if mx <= 0:
       w=np.ones(m)
    else:
       cc=cc/mx
       if power >= 1.0:
          w=cc ** power
       else:
          w=np.exp(power*np.log(np.maximum(cc,1e-300)))
    if rng is not None:
       w = w + 1e-9 * rng.random(m)
    s=float(np.sum(w))
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
       w=np.ones(m)/m
    else:
       w=w/s
    return w
```

`_improve_mirror` code with careful variables.
Let's write it:
```
def _improve_mirror(a_init, rng, time_limit, p_start=3.0, p_end=10.0, step_init=0.18, noise_init=0.04):
    a = _clip_array(a_init)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a, np.inf
    if time_limit <= 0:
        return a, _fast_score(a)
    S = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * n))
    a = _scale_to(a, S)
    if a is None:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) * (S / n)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    a = _scale_to(a, S)
    Nc = _next_pow2(2*n - 1)
    Nh = _next_pow2(3*n - 2)
    z = np.log(a)
    z -= float(np.mean(z))
    a = np.exp(z)
    a = _scale_to(a, S)
    current_score = _fast_max(a, Nc)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = current_score
    step = step_init
    noise = noise_init
    power = p_start
    no_improve = 0
    iteration = 0
    end = time.monotonic() + float(time_limit)
    while time.monotonic() < end:
       iteration += 1
       c = _fast_convolution(a, Nc)
       cur = float(np.max(c))
       if cur < best_score - 1e-12:
          best = a.copy(); best_score = cur
          if iteration % 100 == 0 or best_score < TARGET_SCORE + 0.05:
              print(f"[mirror] n={n} iter={iteration} best_score={best_score:.7f}")
       if abs(cur - current_score) > 1e-9:
          current_score = cur
       if power < p_end:
          power = min(p_end, power + 0.004)
       decay = min(1.0, iteration / 4000.0)
       temp = 0.12 * (1.0 - 0.5 * decay)
       noise = max(0.003, noise_init * (1.0 - 0.5 * decay))
       w = _make_weights(c, power, rng)
       H = _hankel_active(a, w, Nh)
       mu = float(np.dot(a, H)) / S
       g = H - mu
       maxg = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
       if maxg < 1e-13:
           z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-12)); z -= float(np.mean(z))
           z += 0.1 * rng.standard_normal(n)
           z = np.clip(z, -25.0, 25.0); z -= float(np.mean(z))
           a = _scale_to(np.exp(z), S)
           if a is None:
              a = best.copy()
           z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-12)); z -= float(np.mean(z))
           current_score = _fast_max(a, Nc)
           step = step_init
           no_improve = 0
           continue
       direction = g / maxg
       nv = noise * rng.standard_normal(n)
       old_current = current_score
       best_trial = None
       best_trial_score = np.inf
       alphas = (step, step*0.5, step*0.25, step*0.125, step*0.0625)
       for alpha in alphas:
           zc = z - alpha * direction + nv
           zc = np.clip(zc, -25.0, 25.0)
           zc -= float(np.mean(zc))
           cand = np.exp(zc)
           cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
           cand = _scale_to(cand, S)
           if cand is None:
               continue
           sc = _fast_max(cand, Nc)
           if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_trial_score:
               best_trial_score = sc
               best_trial = cand
       if best_trial is not None:
           threshold = old_current + temp * max(1.0, float(best_score))
           if best_trial_score < threshold:
               a = best_trial
               current_score = best_trial_score
               if current_score < old_current:
                   no_improve = 0
               else:
                   no_improve += 1
               if current_score < best_score:
                   no_improve = 0
                   best = a.copy(); best_score = current_score
                   print(f"[mirror] n={n} improved best={best_score:.7f}")
               z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-12)); z -= float(np.mean(z))
               step = min(0.55, step * 1.02)
               if current_score > best_score * 1.25 and no_improve > 15:
                   a = best.copy(); z = np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-12)); z-=mean; current_score=best_score; no_improve=0; step=step_init
               continue
       step *= 0.75
       no_improve += 1
       if no_improve > 45:
           kick = 0.08 * min(5, no_improve // 10 + 1)
           base = best if current_score > best_score * 1.2 else a
           z = np.log(np.maximum(base, 1e-12)); z -= float(np.mean(z))
           z += kick * rng.standard_normal(n)
           z = np.clip(z, -25.0, 25.0); z -= float(np.mean(z))
           a = _scale_to(np.exp(z), S)
           if a is None:
               a = best.copy()
           z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-12)); z -= float(np.mean(z))
           current_score = _fast_max(a, Nc)
           step = step_init
           no_improve = 0
    final_score = _fast_score(best)
    return best, final_score
```
Need ensure `noise` not shadow? okay.

Potential bug: If accepted worse (worse than best but within temp), no_improve increments; after 15 and current >1.25best, snap. Good.

Now LP helpers.

`_convolve_matrix_rows`:
```
def _convolve_matrix_rows(a, idx):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    A = np.zeros((idx.size, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for r in range(idx.size):
        l = int(idx[r])
        if l < n:
            A[r, :l+1] = a[l::-1]
        else:
            start = l - n + 1
            if start < n:
                A[r, start:n] = a[n-1:start-1:-1]
    return A
```
If idx size 0, returns shape (0,n). okay.

`_lp_direction_from_rows`:
```
def _lp_direction_from_rows(a, idx, M, rng, S):
    a = np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    if n==0 or idx.size==0: return None
    if linprog is None:
        x = 1.0 / (np.maximum(a,1e-12) + 0.05*(S/float(n)))
    else:
        try:
            A = _convolve_matrix_rows(a, idx)
            b_ub = float(max(M, 1e-9)) * np.ones(A.shape[0], dtype=np.float64)
            c_obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
            cap = float(S)
            bounds = [(0.0, cap)] * n
            res = linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve': True, 'max_iter': 20000})
            x = None
            if res is not None and getattr(res, 'x', None) is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
                x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        except Exception:
            x = None
        if x is None:
            x = 1.0 / (np.maximum(a,1e-12) + 0.05*(S/float(n)))
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, S)
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=S, neginf=0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(x))
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    g = x * (S / s)
    if rng is not None:
        g = g * np.exp(0.02 * rng.standard_normal(n))
    g = _scale_to(g, S)
    return g
```
If `S` maybe 0? no.

Could using `cap=S` in bounds cause if x entries sum S and one cap S, okay. LP may push all to cap? normalized. Good.

`_improve_lp` as above. Need use `_fast_convolution` and `idx` in loop. If c values finite. If c contains nan? no.

```
def _improve_lp(a_init, rng, time_limit, top_constraints=None):
    a = _clip_array(a_init)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0: return a, np.inf
    if n > 1500 or time_limit <=0:
       return a, _fast_score(a)
    S = sqrt
    Nc = next
    a=max(a,1e-12); a=scale
    current_score = _fast_max(a,Nc)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=current_score
    no_improve=0
    end=...
    if top_constraints is None:
       if n <= 512: top_constraints=2*n-1
       else: top_constraints=min(2*n-1, max(256, n//2))
    while time<end:
       c=_fast_convolution(a,Nc)
       cur=max
       if cur<best_score: ...
       if top_constraints < 2*n-1:
          idx=np.argpartition(c, -int(top_constraints))[-int(top_constraints):]
       else:
          idx=np.arange(2*n-1)
       g=_lp_direction_from_rows(a, idx, cur, rng, S)
       accepted=False
       if g is not None:
          best_cand=None; best_sc=cur
          lams = (0.35,0.18,0.09,0.05,0.025,0.015,0.008,0.004,0.002,0.001)
          for lam in lams:
             cand = (1.0-lam)*a + lam*g
             cand=np.maximum(cand,0.0)
             cand=_scale_to(cand,S)
             if cand is None: continue
             sc=_fast_max(cand,Nc)
             if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc=sc; best_cand=cand
          if best_cand is not None and best_sc < cur - 1e-12:
             a=best_cand; current_score=best_sc; no_improve=0; accepted=True
             if current_score < best_score:
                best=a.copy(); best_score=current_score; print
       if not accepted:
          no_improve +=1
          if no_improve > 6:
             amp=0.08+0.02*min(4,no_improve//6)
             base = best if current_score > best_score*1.15 else a
             z=np.log(np.maximum(base,1e-12)); z-=mean; z+=amp*rng.normal; clip; a=scale; z=...; current_score=fastmax; no_improve=0
          else:
             z=... current + small noise; a=scale; z=...; current_score=fastmax
             if current_score < best_score: best=... maybe not
    final=_fast_score(best)
    return best, final_score
```
If `best_sc` is lower but we only accept `< cur - 1e-12`; if best_sc not lower no. Good.

Potential: LP direction `g` may be same as a often; line search no improve. Restart.

Now start helpers:
```
def _random_walk_start(n, rng):
    z=np.zeros(n)
    step = 0.7
    for i in range(1,n):
       z[i]=z[i-1]+float(rng.normal(0.0, step))
    z=np.clip(z,-3,3)
    z-=z.mean()
    return np.exp(0.7*z)
```
Using loop O(n) but n small. For n=8192, okay. Could vectorize cumsum:
```
z=np.cumsum(rng.normal(0,0.7,n)); z = np.clip(z, -3,3); z-=z.mean(); return np.exp(0.7*z)
```
Faster. Use cumul.

`_smooth_start`:
```
t=np.arange(n, dtype=float)
a=np.ones(n)
for _ in range(4):
    f=float(rng.integers(1,9))+0.5
    phi=float(rng.uniform(-np.pi,np.pi))
    amp=float(rng.uniform(0.15,0.7))
    a *= np.exp(amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*f*t/float(n)+phi))
return np.maximum(a,1e-3)
```

`_block_start`:
```
block=max(4,n//256)
m=(n+block-1)//block
vals=np.exp(0.5*rng.normal(0,1,m))
out=np.empty(n)
out[:] = np.repeat(vals, block)[:n]
return np.maximum(out,1e-3)
```

base=0.15
return base + (1.0-base)*(rng.integers(0,2,n,dtype=np.float64) if rng has? `rng.random(n) < 0.5`)
```

a=np.ones(n)
if n%2==0:
   half=n//2
   left=0.15 + 1.7*rng.random(half)
   a[:half]=left
   a[half:]=2.0-left[::-1]
else:
   half=n//2
   left=0.15+1.7*rng.random(half)
   a[:half]=left
   a[half:] = 2.0 - left[::-1]
   a[n//2]=0.2
return np.maximum(a,0.05)
```

`_interpolate_sequence`:
```
def _interpolate_sequence(a, n_new):
    a=_clip_array(a)
    if a.size == n_new: return a.copy()
    if a.size ==0: return np.ones(n_new)
    if a.size ==1: return np.full(n_new,float(a[0]))
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,a.size)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n_new)
    b=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
    b=np.maximum(b,1e-6)
    return b
```

`_generate_starts(n,rng,parent=None)`:
```
def _generate_starts(n, rng, parent=None):
    starts=[]
    if parent is not None and parent.size>0:
       p=_interpolate_sequence(parent,n)
       starts.append(p)
    starts.append(np.ones(n))
    for _ in range(2):
       starts.append(_random_walk_start(n,rng))
       starts.append(_smooth_start(n,rng))
       starts.append(_block_start(n,rng))
       starts.append(_binary_start(n,rng))
       starts.append(_anti_palindromic_start(n,rng))
    return starts
```
For n=1024, painting many starts okay.

`_add_candidate` and `_sort_candidates`:
```
def _add_candidate(cands, cand, score, max_keep=8):
    if cand is None or not np.isfinite(score): return cands
    arr = _clip_array(cand)
    if arr.size == 0 or np.sum(arr) <= 1e-12: return cands
    # remove duplicates with same size and similar? no
    item = (arr.copy(), float(score))
    if len(cands) >= max_keep:
        worst = 0; worst_score = cands[0][1]
        for i in range(1, len(cands)):
            if cands[i][1] > worst_score:
                worst = i; worst_score = cands[i][1]
        if score >= worst_score:
            return cands
        cands.pop(worst)
    cands.append(item)
    return cands
```
If score=inf? check finite. Good.

`_sort_candidates`:
```
def _sort_candidates(cands):
    arr=list(cands)
    m=len(arr)
    for i in range(m-1):
       minj=i; minsc=arr[i][1]
       for j in range(i+1,m):
          if arr[j][1] < minsc:
             minj=j; minsc=arr[j][1]
       if minj!=i:
          tmp=arr[i]; arr[i]=arr[minj]; arr[minj]=tmp
    return arr
```

`_exact_check`:
```
def _exact_check(seq, max_len=8192):
    arr = _clip_array(seq)
    if arr.size ==0 or arr.size > max_len:
       return np.inf
    if np.sum(arr) <= 1e-300:
       return np.inf
    try:
       val = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in arr])
    except Exception:
       return np.inf
    if val is None or not np.isfinite(val): return np.inf
    return float(val)
```

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 6.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) if seed is not None else 42)
    cands = []
    best_seq = None
    best_score = np.inf

    parent = _get_parent()

        n_main = int(parent.size)
    else:
        n_main = 4096

    starts = _generate_starts(n_main, rng, parent)
    Nc_main = _next_pow2(2*n_main-1)
    best_start=None; best_start_score=np.inf

    cands = _add_candidate(cands, best_start, best_start_score, 8)
    if best_start_score < best_score:
       best_seq = best_start.copy(); best_score = best_start_score
       print("[init] best start", best_score)

    # Phase 1 LP 512

       n_lp=512

          if st is None: st=np.ones(n_lp)*(S_lp/n_lp)

             lp_start_score=sc; lp_best=st.copy()
       if lp_best is not None:
          cands=_add_candidate(cands, lp_best, lp_start_score, 8)
          if lp_start_score < best_score:
             best_seq=lp_best.copy(); best_score=lp_start_score
          tl = min(_time_left(deadline), 450.0)
          seq,score=_improve_lp(lp_best, rng, tl, top_constraints=2*n_lp-1)
          cands=_add_candidate(cands, seq, score, 8)
          if score < best_score:
             best_seq=seq.copy(); best_score=score; print
    # Phase 2 LP 1024 if time and maybe linprog or fallback
    if _time_left(deadline) > 300:
       n_lp=1024
       S_lp=...
       lp_starts=_generate_starts(n_lp, rng, parent) # parent downscaled
       choose start similarly
       tl = min(_time_left(deadline), 300.0)
       seq,score=_improve_lp(lp_best, rng, tl, top_constraints=512)
       update
    # Phase 3 remaining mirror on best
    rem=_time_left(deadline)
    if rem > 120:
       base = best_seq if best_seq is not None else best_start
       tl = min(rem - 20.0, 900.0)
       seq,score=_improve_mirror(base, rng, tl)
       cands=_add_candidate(cands, seq, score, 8)
       if score < best_score: update
    # Phase 4 if still time, mirror on upsampled/new
    rem=_time_left(deadline)
    if rem > 250:
       base = best_seq if best_seq is not None else best_start
       if base.size != n_main:
          base = _interpolate_sequence(base, n_main)
       base = _scale_to(base, S_main)
       if base is None: base=np.ones(n_main)*(S_main/n_main)
       tl = min(rem - 30.0, 700.0)
       seq,score=_improve_mirror(base, rng, tl)
       update
       rem=_time_left(deadline)
       if rem > 200:
          fresh = _scale_to(_random_walk_start(n_main, rng), S_main)
          if fresh is None: fresh=np.ones(n_main)*(S_main/n_main)
          seq,score=_improve_mirror(fresh, rng, rem - 30.0)
          update
    # final exact choose
    chosen_seq = None; chosen_score = np.inf
    top = _sort_candidates(cands)
    checked=0
    for item in top:
       if _time_left(deadline) <= 12 or checked >= 4: break
       if item[0].size > 8192: continue
       es = _exact_check(item[0], max_len=8192)
       checked +=1
       if np.isfinite(es):
           if chosen_seq is None or es < chosen_score:
              chosen_seq=item[0].copy(); chosen_score=es
       # If we found very good exact (< target), can still continue but maybe break? Continue for lower.
    if chosen_seq is None:
       if best_seq is not None:
          chosen_seq=best_seq
       else:
          chosen_seq=np.ones(256)
    # Final exact verify chosen if possible and time
    chosen_arr = _clip_array(chosen_seq)
    if chosen_arr.size > 0 and chosen_arr.size <= 10000 and _time_left(deadline) > 5:
       es = _exact_check(chosen_arr, max_len=10000)
       if np.isfinite(es):
          # no change needed, but if we have another exact candidate? not needed
          pass
    # fallback if invalid
    if chosen_arr.size == 0 or np.sum(chosen_arr) <= 1e-12:
       chosen_arr = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)
    return [float(x) for x in chosen_arr]

```python
"""
Search for non-negative sequences minimizing the step-function convolution objective.

The implementation uses:
- FFT-based evaluations of the objective for fast local search;
- a mirror/gradient peak-shaving local search on log-height parameters;
- LP-direction iterations inspired by the previous state-of-the-art method:
  for fixed a, solve  maximize sum(b)  s.t.  b >= 0,  conv(a,b) <= M,
  then blend a with the normalized LP direction;
- multiple starting points and lengths, with exact evaluate_sequence checks near the end.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None

TARGET_SCORE = 1.5027


def _time_left(deadline):
    return float(max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic()))


def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    p = 1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def _clip_array(x):
    arr = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return arr
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return arr


def _scale_to(a, target_sum):
    a = _clip_array(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return a * (float(target_sum) / s)


def _fast_convolution(a, size):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, n=size)
    return c[:2 * n - 1]


def _fast_max(a, size=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    if size is None or int(size) < 2 * n - 1:
        size = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    c = _fast_convolution(a, size)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if m < 0.0 and m > -1e-8:
        m = 0.0
    return m


def _fast_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.inf
    m = _fast_max(a, None)
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def _hankel_active(a, w, size=None):
    """
    For H_i = sum_l w_l a_{l-i}, with a length n and w length 2n-1,
    H = convolve(reverse(a), w)[n-1 : 2n-1].
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if size is None or int(size) < 3 * n - 2:
        size = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    ar = a[::-1]
    fa = np.fft.rfft(ar, n=size)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, n=size)
    d = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw, n=size)
    return d[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def _make_weights(c, power, rng):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    m = c.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    cc = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    mx = float(np.max(cc))
    if mx <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(mx):
        w = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        cc = cc / mx
        if power >= 1.0:
            w = cc ** power
        else:
            w = np.exp(power * np.log(np.maximum(cc, 1e-300)))

    if rng is not None:
        w = w + 1e-9 * rng.random(m)

    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        w = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    else:
        w = w / s
    return w


def _improve_mirror(a_init, rng, time_limit,
                    p_start=3.0, p_end=10.0,
                    step_init=0.18, noise_init=0.04):
    """
    Log-space mirror / stochastic gradient peak shaving.
    Maintains sum(a) = sqrt(2n), for which the objective equals max(conv(a,a)).
    """
    a = _clip_array(a_init)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a, np.inf
    if time_limit <= 0:
        return a, _fast_score(a)

    S = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * n))
    a = _scale_to(a, S)
    if a is None:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) * (S / float(n))

    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    a = _scale_to(a, S)

    Nc = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    Nh = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    z = np.log(a)
    z -= float(np.mean(z))
    a = np.exp(z)
    a = _scale_to(a, S)
    if a is None:
        return _clip_array(a_init), _fast_score(a_init)

    current_score = _fast_max(a, Nc)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = current_score

    step = float(step_init)
    noise = float(noise_init)
    power = float(p_start)
    no_improve = 0
    iteration = 0

    end = time.monotonic() + float(time_limit)

    while time.monotonic() < end:
        iteration += 1

        c = _fast_convolution(a, Nc)
        cur = float(np.max(c))

        if cur < best_score - 1e-12:
            best = a.copy()
            best_score = cur
            if iteration % 100 == 0 or best_score < TARGET_SCORE + 0.05:
                print(f"[mirror] n={n} iter={iteration} best_score={best_score:.7f}")

        if abs(cur - current_score) > 1e-9:
            current_score = cur

        if power < p_end:
            power = min(p_end, power + 0.004)

        decay = min(1.0, iteration / 4000.0)
        temp = 0.12 * (1.0 - 0.5 * decay)
        noise = max(0.003, float(noise_init) * (1.0 - 0.5 * decay))

        w = _make_weights(c, power, rng)
        H = _hankel_active(a, w, Nh)
        mu = float(np.dot(a, H)) / S
        g = H - mu
        maxg = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))

        if maxg < 1e-13:
            z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-12))
            z -= float(np.mean(z))
            z += 0.1 * rng.standard_normal(n)
            z = np.clip(z, -25.0, 25.0)
            z -= float(np.mean(z))
            a = _scale_to(np.exp(z), S)
            if a is None:
                a = best.copy()
            z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-12))
            z -= float(np.mean(z))
            current_score = _fast_max(a, Nc)
            step = float(step_init)
            no_improve = 0
            continue

        direction = g / maxg
        nv = noise * rng.standard_normal(n)

        old_current = float(current_score)
        best_trial = None
        best_trial_score = np.inf

        alphas = (step, step * 0.5, step * 0.25, step * 0.125, step * 0.0625)
        for alpha in alphas:
            zc = z - alpha * direction + nv
            zc = np.clip(zc, -25.0, 25.0)
            zc -= float(np.mean(zc))
            cand = np.exp(zc)
            cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
            cand = _scale_to(cand, S)
            if cand is None:
                continue
            sc = _fast_max(cand, Nc)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_trial_score:
                best_trial_score = sc
                best_trial = cand

        if best_trial is not None:
            threshold = old_current + temp * max(1.0, float(best_score))
            if best_trial_score < threshold:
                a = best_trial
                current_score = best_trial_score

                if current_score < old_current:
                    no_improve = 0
                else:
                    no_improve += 1

                if current_score < best_score:
                    no_improve = 0
                    best = a.copy()
                    best_score = current_score
                    print(f"[mirror] n={n} improved best={best_score:.7f}")

                z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-12))
                z -= float(np.mean(z))

                step = min(0.55, step * 1.02)

                if current_score > best_score * 1.25 and no_improve > 15:
                    a = best.copy()
                    z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-12))
                    z -= float(np.mean(z))
                    current_score = float(best_score)
                    no_improve = 0
                    step = float(step_init)
                continue

        step *= 0.75
        no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > 45:
            kick_amp = 0.08 * min(5.0, float(no_improve // 10 + 1))
            base = best if current_score > best_score * 1.2 else a
            z = np.log(np.maximum(base, 1e-12))
            z -= float(np.mean(z))
            z += kick_amp * rng.standard_normal(n)
            z = np.clip(z, -25.0, 25.0)
            z -= float(np.mean(z))
            a = _scale_to(np.exp(z), S)
            if a is None:
                a = best.copy()
            z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-12))
            z -= float(np.mean(z))
            current_score = _fast_max(a, Nc)
            step = float(step_init)
            no_improve = 0

    final_score = _fast_score(best)
    return best, final_score


def _convolve_matrix_rows(a, idx):
    """Rows of the convolution matrix a * b. Each selected row is conv(a,b) at lag idx."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    idx = np.asarray(idx, dtype=np.int64)
    A = np.zeros((idx.size, n), dtype=np.float64)

    for r in range(idx.size):
        l = int(idx[r])
        if l < n:
            A[r, :l + 1] = a[l::-1]
        else:
            start = l - n + 1
            if start < n:
                A[r, start:n] = a[n - 1:start - 1:-1]

    return A


def _lp_direction_from_rows(a, idx, M, rng, S):
    """
    LP direction inspired by the SOTA description:
        maximize sum(b)
        subject to b >= 0, conv(a,b) <= M.
    Fallback is inverse-a direction if the LP is unavailable or fails.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0 or idx.size == 0:
        return None

    x = None
    if linprog is not None:
        try:
            A = _convolve_matrix_rows(a, idx)
            b_ub = float(max(M, 1e-9)) * np.ones(A.shape[0], dtype=np.float64)
            c_obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
            cap = float(S)
            bounds = [(0.0, cap)] * n
            res = linprog(
                c_obj,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=b_ub,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
                options={"presolve": True, "max_iter": 10000}
            )
            if (res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None
                    and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x))):
                x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        except Exception:
            x = None

    if x is None:
        # Stable heuristic: put more weight where current a is small.
        x = 1.0 / (np.maximum(a, 1e-12) + 0.05 * (S / float(n)))

    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, S)
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=S, neginf=0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(x))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return None

    g = x * (S / s)
    if rng is not None:
        g = g * np.exp(0.02 * rng.standard_normal(n))
    g = _scale_to(g, S)
    return g


def _improve_lp(a_init, rng, time_limit, top_constraints=None):
    """
    LP-direction local search for modest n.
    Uses the same normalization sum(a) = sqrt(2n), so objective = max conv.
    """
    a = _clip_array(a_init)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a, np.inf
    if n > 1500 or time_limit <= 0:
        return a, _fast_score(a)

    S = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * n))
    Nc = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)

    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    a = _scale_to(a, S)
    if a is None:
        return _clip_array(a_init), _fast_score(a_init)

    current_score = _fast_max(a, Nc)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = current_score

    no_improve = 0
    end = time.monotonic() + float(time_limit)

    if top_constraints is None:
        if n <= 512:
            top_constraints = 2 * n - 1
        else:
            top_constraints = min(2 * n - 1, max(256, n // 2))
    top_constraints = int(max(16, min(top_constraints, 2 * n - 1)))

    while time.monotonic() < end:
        c = _fast_convolution(a, Nc)
        cur = float(np.max(c))

        if cur < best_score - 1e-12:
            best = a.copy()
            best_score = cur
            print(f"[lp] n={n} best_score={best_score:.7f}")

        if abs(cur - current_score) > 1e-9:
            current_score = cur

        if top_constraints < 2 * n - 1:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, -top_constraints)[-top_constraints:]
        else:
            idx = np.arange(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.int64)

        g = _lp_direction_from_rows(a, idx, current_score, rng, S)
        accepted = False

        if g is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            best_cand = None
            best_sc = float(current_score)

            lams = (0.35, 0.18, 0.09, 0.05, 0.025, 0.015, 0.008, 0.004, 0.002, 0.001)
            for lam in lams:
                cand = (1.0 - lam) * a + lam * g
                cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
                cand = _scale_to(cand, S)
                if cand is None:
                    continue
                sc = _fast_max(cand, Nc)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_cand = cand

            if best_cand is not None and best_sc < current_score - 1e-12:
                a = best_cand
                current_score = best_sc
                no_improve = 0
                accepted = True
                if current_score < best_score:
                    best = a.copy()
                    best_score = current_score
                    print(f"[lp] n={n} improved best={best_score:.7f}")

        if not accepted:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 6:
                amp = 0.08 + 0.02 * min(4.0, float(no_improve // 6))
                base = best if current_score > best_score * 1.15 else a
                z = np.log(np.maximum(base, 1e-12))
                z -= float(np.mean(z))
                z += amp * rng.standard_normal(n)
                z = np.clip(z, -25.0, 25.0)
                z -= float(np.mean(z))
                a = _scale_to(np.exp(z), S)
                if a is None:
                    a = best.copy()
                z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-12))
                z -= float(np.mean(z))
                current_score = _fast_max(a, Nc)
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-12))
                z -= float(np.mean(z))
                z += 0.04 * rng.standard_normal(n)
                z = np.clip(z, -25.0, 25.0)
                z -= float(np.mean(z))
                a = _scale_to(np.exp(z), S)
                if a is None:
                    a = best.copy()
                z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-12))
                z -= float(np.mean(z))
                current_score = _fast_max(a, Nc)
                if current_score < best_score:
                    best = a.copy()
                    best_score = current_score

    final_score = _fast_score(best)
    return best, final_score


def _random_walk_start(n, rng):
    z = np.cumsum(rng.normal(0.0, 0.7, size=n))
    z = np.clip(z, -3.0, 3.0)
    z -= float(np.mean(z))
    return np.exp(0.7 * z)


def _smooth_start(n, rng):
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for _ in range(4):
        f = float(rng.integers(1, 9)) + 0.5
        phi = float(rng.uniform(-np.pi, np.pi))
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.15, 0.7))
        a *= np.exp(amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * t / float(n) + phi))
    return np.maximum(a, 1e-3)


def _block_start(n, rng):
    block = max(4, n // 256)
    m = (n + block - 1) // block
    vals = np.exp(0.5 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=m))
    out = np.repeat(vals, block)[:n]
    return np.maximum(out, 1e-3)


def _binary_start(n, rng):
    vals = rng.random(n)
    return np.where(vals < 0.5, 0.12, 1.15)


def _anti_palindromic_start(n, rng):
    a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    half = n // 2
    left = 0.15 + 1.7 * rng.random(half)
    if half > 0:
        a[:half] = left
        a[half:] = 2.0 - left[::-1]
    if n % 2 == 1:
        a[half] = 0.2
    return np.maximum(a, 0.05)


def _interpolate_sequence(a, n_new):
    a = _clip_array(a)
    n_new = int(n_new)
    if a.size == n_new:
        return a.copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n_new, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 1:
        return np.full(n_new, float(a[0]), dtype=np.float64)

    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_new)
    b = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return np.maximum(b, 1e-6)


def _generate_starts(n, rng, parent=None):
    starts = []
    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        p = _interpolate_sequence(parent, n)
        starts.append(p)

    starts.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    for _ in range(2):
        starts.append(_random_walk_start(n, rng))
        starts.append(_smooth_start(n, rng))
        starts.append(_block_start(n, rng))
        starts.append(_binary_start(n, rng))
        starts.append(_anti_palindromic_start(n, rng))

    return starts


def _get_parent_sequence():
    try:
        hs = height_sequence_1
    except Exception:
        return None
    arr = _clip_array(hs)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    s = float(np.sum(arr))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    return arr


def _choose_best_start(starts, target_sum, size):
    best = None
    best_score = np.inf
    for st in starts:
        st = _scale_to(st, target_sum)
        if st is None:
            continue
        sc = _fast_max(st, size)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best = st.copy()
            best_score = float(sc)
    if best is None:
        best = np.ones(int(max(1, len(starts[0]))), dtype=np.float64) if starts else np.ones(256)
        best_score = _fast_score(best)
    return best, best_score


def _add_candidate(cands, cand, score, max_keep=8):
    if cand is None or not np.isfinite(score):
        return cands
    arr = _clip_array(cand)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return cands
    s = float(np.sum(arr))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return cands

    item = (arr.copy(), float(score))

    if len(cands) >= max_keep:
        worst = 0
        worst_score = cands[0][1]
        for i in range(1, len(cands)):
            if cands[i][1] > worst_score:
                worst = i
                worst_score = cands[i][1]
        if score >= worst_score:
            return cands
        cands.pop(worst)

    cands.append(item)
    return cands


def _sort_candidates(cands):
    arr = list(cands)
    m = len(arr)
    for i in range(m - 1):
        minj = i
        minsc = arr[i][1]
        for j in range(i + 1, m):
            if arr[j][1] < minsc:
                minj = j
                minsc = arr[j][1]
        if minj != i:
            tmp = arr[i]
            arr[i] = arr[minj]
            arr[minj] = tmp
    return arr


def _exact_check(seq, max_len=8192):
    arr = _clip_array(seq)
    if arr.size == 0 or arr.size > max_len:
        return np.inf
    s = float(np.sum(arr))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.inf
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in arr])
    except Exception:
        return np.inf
    if val is None:
        return np.inf
    val = float(val)
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return np.inf
    return val


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 6.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) if seed is not None else 42)

    cands = []
    best_seq = None
    best_score = np.inf

    parent = _get_parent_sequence()

    if parent is not None and parent.size >= 2048 and parent.size <= 8192:
        n_main = int(parent.size)
    else:
        n_main = 4096

    S_main = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * n_main))
    Nc_main = _next_pow2(2 * n_main - 1)

    main_starts = _generate_starts(n_main, rng, parent)
    best_main, best_main_score = _choose_best_start(main_starts, S_main, Nc_main)
    cands = _add_candidate(cands, best_main, best_main_score, 8)
    if best_main_score < best_score:
        best_seq = best_main.copy()
        best_score = best_main_score
        print(f"[init main] n={n_main} best_score={best_score:.7f}")

    # Phase 1: small LP path. This is often the strongest structured local search.
    if _time_left(deadline) > 120 and linprog is not None:
        n_lp = 512
        S_lp = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * n_lp))
        Nc_lp = _next_pow2(2 * n_lp - 1)
        lp_starts = _generate_starts(n_lp, rng, parent)
        lp_best, lp_best_score = _choose_best_start(lp_starts, S_lp, Nc_lp)

        cands = _add_candidate(cands, lp_best, lp_best_score, 8)
        if lp_best_score < best_score:
            best_seq = lp_best.copy()
            best_score = lp_best_score
            print(f"[init lp512] best_score={best_score:.7f}")

        tl = min(_time_left(deadline), 450.0)
        seq, score = _improve_lp(lp_best, rng, tl, top_constraints=2 * n_lp - 1)
        cands = _add_candidate(cands, seq, score, 8)
        if score < best_score:
            best_seq = seq.copy()
            best_score = score
            print(f"[lp512 done] best_score={best_score:.7f}")

    # Phase 2: medium LP path for more structure / larger degrees of freedom.
    if _time_left(deadline) > 320 and linprog is not None:
        n_lp = 1024
        S_lp = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * n_lp))
        Nc_lp = _next_pow2(2 * n_lp - 1)
        lp_starts = _generate_starts(n_lp, rng, parent)
        lp_best, lp_best_score = _choose_best_start(lp_starts, S_lp, Nc_lp)

        cands = _add_candidate(cands, lp_best, lp_best_score, 8)
        if lp_best_score < best_score:
            best_seq = lp_best.copy()
            best_score = lp_best_score
            print(f"[init lp1024] best_score={best_score:.7f}")

        tl = min(_time_left(deadline), 320.0)
        seq, score = _improve_lp(lp_best, rng, tl, top_constraints=512)
        cands = _add_candidate(cands, seq, score, 8)
        if score < best_score:
            best_seq = seq.copy()
            best_score = score
            print(f"[lp1024 done] best_score={best_score:.7f}")

    # Phase 3: polished mirror search from the current best candidate.
    rem = _time_left(deadline)
    if rem > 120:
        base = best_seq if best_seq is not None else best_main
        tl = max(0.0, min(rem - 20.0, 900.0))
        seq, score = _improve_mirror(base, rng, tl)
        cands = _add_candidate(cands, seq, score, 8)
        if score < best_score:
            best_seq = seq.copy()
            best_score = score
            print(f"[mirror best done] best_score={best_score:.7f}")

    # Phase 4: if useful, try a longer / re-sampled variant of the best sequence.
    rem = _time_left(deadline)
    if rem > 260:
        base = best_seq if best_seq is not None else best_main
        if base.size != n_main:
            base = _interpolate_sequence(base, n_main)
        base = _scale_to(base, S_main)
        if base is None:
            base = np.ones(n_main, dtype=np.float64) * (S_main / float(n_main))

        tl = max(0.0, min(rem - 30.0, 700.0))
        seq, score = _improve_mirror(base, rng, tl)
        cands = _add_candidate(cands, seq, score, 8)
        if score < best_score:
            best_seq = seq.copy()
            best_score = score
            print(f"[mirror resampled done] best_score={best_score:.7f}")

        rem = _time_left(deadline)
        if rem > 220:
            fresh = _scale_to(_random_walk_start(n_main, rng), S_main)
            if fresh is None:
                fresh = np.ones(n_main, dtype=np.float64) * (S_main / float(n_main))
            seq, score = _improve_mirror(fresh, rng, max(0.0, rem - 30.0))
            cands = _add_candidate(cands, seq, score, 8)
            if score < best_score:
                best_seq = seq.copy()
                best_score = score
                print(f"[mirror fresh done] best_score={best_score:.7f}")

    # Final selection: exact-check the few strongest internal candidates.
    chosen_seq = None
    chosen_exact = np.inf
    top = _sort_candidates(cands)
    checked = 0

    for item in top:
        if _time_left(deadline) <= 12 or checked >= 5:
            break
        if item[0].size > 8192:
            continue
        es = _exact_check(item[0], max_len=8192)
        checked += 1
        if np.isfinite(es):
            if chosen_seq is None or es < chosen_exact:
                chosen_seq = item[0].copy()
                chosen_exact = es

    if chosen_seq is None:
        if best_seq is not None:
            chosen_seq = best_seq
        else:
            chosen_seq = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)

    chosen_arr = _clip_array(chosen_seq)

    # One final exact verification if affordable.
    if chosen_arr.size > 0 and chosen_arr.size <= 12000 and _time_left(deadline) > 6:
        es = _exact_check(chosen_arr, max_len=12000)
        if np.isfinite(es):
            chosen_exact = es

    if chosen_arr.size == 0 or float(np.sum(chosen_arr)) <= 1e-12:
        chosen_arr = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)

    return [float(x) for x in chosen_arr]
```
